def validate_stop_request_C12(ws_check_number: int, ws_check_already_cleared: str, ws_stop_valid: str, ws_stop_reject: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_stop_valid = 'Y'
    if ws_check_number == 0:
        ws_stop_valid = 'N'
        ws_stop_reject = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
    if ws_check_already_cleared == 'Y':
        ws_stop_valid = 'N'
        ws_stop_reject = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'
    return ws_stop_valid, ws_stop_reject

def create_stop_order_C12(acct_id: str, ws_check_number: int, ws_check_amount: Decimal, ws_payee_name: str, ws_process_date: int) -> WsStopRecordC12:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_stop_record = WsStopRecordC12()
    ws_stop_record.stop_account = acct_id
    ws_stop_record.stop_check_number = ws_check_number
    ws_stop_record.stop_amount = ws_check_amount
    ws_stop_record.stop_payee = ws_payee_name
    ws_stop_record.stop_effective_date = ws_process_date
    ws_stop_record.stop_expiry_date = ws_process_date + 180  # integer_of_date is implicit
    ws_stop_record.stop_status = 'A'
    return ws_stop_record

def apply_stop_fee_C12(ws_stop_payment_fee: Decimal, ws_account_balance: Decimal, update_account: callable, send_notification: callable, ws_check_number: int) -> tuple[Decimal, str, str, str]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_account_balance -= ws_stop_payment_fee
    update_account()
    ws_notif_type = 'stop_payment'
    ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = f'Stop payment placed on check #{ws_check_number}'
    send_notification()
    return ws_account_balance, ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject

def safe_deposit_box_C12(box_rental: callable, box_access: callable, box_drilling: callable, box_billing: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    box_rental()
    box_access()
    box_drilling()
    box_billing()

def box_rental_C12(ws_rental_request: str, check_availability: callable, assign_box: callable, create_rental_agreement: callable, ws_box_available: str) -> str:
    """COBOL logic"""
    if ws_rental_request == 'Y':
        ws_box_available = check_availability()
        if ws_box_available == 'Y':
            assign_box()
            create_rental_agreement()
    return ws_box_available

def check_availability_C12(ws_total_boxes: int, ws_requested_size: str, box_status: list[str], box_size: list[str], ws_box_idx: int, ws_box_available: str, ws_assigned_box: int) -> tuple[str, int]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_box_available = 'N'
    ws_assigned_box = 0
    for ws_box_idx in range(ws_total_boxes):
        if box_status[ws_box_idx] == 'A':
            if box_size[ws_box_idx] == ws_requested_size:
                ws_box_available = 'Y'
                ws_assigned_box = ws_box_idx + 1
                break
    return ws_box_available, ws_assigned_box

def assign_box_C12(ws_assigned_box: int, ws_customer_id: str, ws_process_date: int, box_status: list[str], box_renter: list[str], box_rental_date: list[int]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str], list[int]]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    box_status[ws_assigned_box - 1] = 'R'
    box_renter[ws_assigned_box - 1] = ws_customer_id
    box_rental_date[ws_assigned_box - 1] = ws_process_date
    return box_status, box_renter, box_rental_date

def create_rental_agreement_C12(ws_assigned_box: int, ws_customer_id: str, ws_process_date: int, ws_box_size_fee: list[Decimal], ws_requested_size: int) -> WsRentalAgreementC12:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_rental_agreement = WsRentalAgreementC12()
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_box_number = ws_assigned_box
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_customer = ws_customer_id
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_start_date = ws_process_date
    ws_rental_agreement.rental_annual_fee = ws_box_size_fee[ws_requested_size - 1]
    return ws_rental_agreement

def box_access_C12(ws_access_request: str, verify_renter: callable, log_access: callable, escort_to_vault: callable, ws_renter_verified: str) -> str:
    """COBOL logic"""
    if ws_access_request == 'Y':
        ws_renter_verified = verify_renter()
        if ws_renter_verified == 'Y':
            log_access()
            escort_to_vault()
    return ws_renter_verified

def verify_renter_C12(ws_box_number: int, ws_customer_id: str, ws_id_verified: str, ws_key_verified: str, box_renter: list[str], ws_renter_verified: str) -> str:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_renter_verified = 'N'
    if box_renter[ws_box_number - 1] == ws_customer_id:
        if ws_id_verified == 'Y':
            if ws_key_verified == 'Y':
                ws_renter_verified = 'Y'
    return ws_renter_verified

def log_access_C12(ws_box_number: int, ws_customer_id: str, ws_process_date: int) -> WsAccessLogC12:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_access_log = WsAccessLogC12()
    ws_access_log.access_box_number = ws_box_number
    ws_access_log.access_customer = ws_customer_id
    ws_access_log.access_date = ws_process_date
    ws_access_log.access_time = datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S")  # Approximate current_time
    ws_access_log.access_type = 'ENTRY'
    return ws_access_log

def escort_to_vault_C12() -> str:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_display_msg = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'
    print(ws_display_msg)
    return ws_display_msg

def box_drilling_C12(ws_drilling_request: str, validate_drilling_auth: callable, schedule_drilling: callable, notify_renter: callable, ws_drilling_authorized: str) -> str:
    """COBOL logic"""
    if ws_drilling_request == 'Y':
        ws_drilling_authorized = validate_drilling_auth()
        if ws_drilling_authorized == 'Y':
            schedule_drilling()
            notify_renter()
    return ws_drilling_authorized

def validate_drilling_auth_C12(ws_rent_delinquent_months: int, ws_court_order: str, ws_deceased_renter: str, ws_executor_verified: str, ws_drilling_authorized: str) -> str:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_drilling_authorized = 'N'
    if ws_rent_delinquent_months >= 12:
        ws_drilling_authorized = 'Y'
    if ws_court_order == 'Y':
        ws_drilling_authorized = 'Y'
    if ws_deceased_renter == 'Y':
        if ws_executor_verified == 'Y':
            ws_drilling_authorized = 'Y'
    return ws_drilling_authorized

def schedule_drilling_C12(ws_box_number: int, ws_drilling_reason: str, ws_process_date: int) -> WsDrillingRecordC12:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_drilling_record = WsDrillingRecordC12()
    ws_drilling_record.drill_box_number = ws_box_number
    ws_drilling_record.drill_reason = ws_drilling_reason
    ws_drilling_record.drill_scheduled_date = ws_process_date + 30  # integer_of_date is implicit
    return ws_drilling_record

def notify_renter_C12(send_notification: callable) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_notif_type = 'box_drilling'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'
    send_notification()
    return ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject

def box_billing_C12(ws_total_boxes: int, box_status: list[str], box_renewal_due: list[str], charge_annual_fee: callable, ws_box_idx: int) -> int:
    """COBOL logic"""
    for ws_box_idx in range(ws_total_boxes):
        if box_status[ws_box_idx] == 'R':
            if box_renewal_due[ws_box_idx] == 'Y':
                charge_annual_fee(ws_box_idx + 1)
    return ws_box_idx

def charge_annual_fee_C12(ws_box_idx: int, ws_account_balance: Decimal, box_renter: list[str], box_annual_fee: list[Decimal], box_next_renewal: list[int], update_account: callable) -> tuple[str, Decimal, list[int]]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_customer_id = box_renter[ws_box_idx - 1]
    ws_fee_amount = box_annual_fee[ws_box_idx - 1]
    ws_account_balance -= ws_fee_amount
    update_account()
    box_next_renewal[ws_box_idx - 1] = box_next_renewal[ws_box_idx - 1] + 10000
    return ws_customer_id, ws_account_balance, box_next_renewal

def merchant_services_C12(process_authorization: callable, capture_transaction: callable, process_settlement: callable, handle_chargeback: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    process_authorization()
    capture_transaction()
    process_settlement()
    handle_chargeback()

def process_authorization_C12(validate_card: callable, check_fraud_score: callable, check_available_credit: callable, approve_auth: callable, decline_auth: callable, ws_card_valid: str, ws_fraud_approved: str, ws_credit_available: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_card_valid = validate_card()
    if ws_card_valid == 'Y':
        ws_fraud_approved = check_fraud_score()
        if ws_fraud_approved == 'Y':
            ws_credit_available = check_available_credit()
            if ws_credit_available == 'Y':
                approve_auth()
            else:
                decline_auth()
        else:
            decline_auth()
    else:
        decline_auth()
    return ws_card_valid, ws_fraud_approved, ws_credit_available

def validate_card_C12(check_luhn: callable, check_expiry: callable, check_cvv: callable, ws_card_valid: str, ws_luhn_valid: str, ws_not_expired: str, ws_cvv_valid: str) -> str:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_card_valid = 'N'
    ws_luhn_valid = check_luhn()
    if ws_luhn_valid == 'Y':
        ws_not_expired = check_expiry()
        if ws_not_expired == 'Y':
            ws_cvv_valid = check_cvv()
            if ws_cvv_valid == 'Y':
                ws_card_valid = 'Y'
    return ws_card_valid

def check_luhn_C12(ws_auth_card_number: str) -> str:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_luhn_sum = 0
    for ws_luhn_idx in range(16, 0, -1):
        ws_luhn_digit = int(ws_auth_card_number[ws_luhn_idx - 1])
        if (17 - ws_luhn_idx) % 2 == 0:
            ws_luhn_digit *= 2
            if ws_luhn_digit > 9:
                ws_luhn_digit -= 9
        ws_luhn_sum += ws_luhn_digit
    if ws_luhn_sum % 10 == 0:
        ws_luhn_valid = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_luhn_valid = 'N'
    return ws_luhn_valid

def check_expiry_C12(ws_auth_expiry_date: int, ws_process_date: int) -> str:
    """COBOL logic"""
    if ws_auth_expiry_date >= ws_process_date:
        ws_not_expired = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_not_expired = 'N'
    return ws_not_expired

def check_cvv_C12(ws_auth_card_number: str, ws_auth_cvv: str, cvvverify: callable) -> str:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_cvv_result = cvvverify(ws_auth_card_number, ws_auth_cvv)
    if ws_cvv_result == 'M':
        ws_cvv_valid = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_cvv_valid = 'N'
    return ws_cvv_valid

def check_fraud_score_C12(ws_auth_request: str, fraudcheck: callable, fraud_score: int, fraud_decline_code: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_fraud_response = fraudcheck(ws_auth_request)  # Assuming fraudcheck returns the entire fraud_response
    if fraud_score < 70:
        ws_fraud_approved = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_fraud_approved = 'N'
        ws_auth_decline_code = fraud_decline_code  # Access fraud_decline_code from fraud_response
    return ws_fraud_approved, ws_auth_decline_code

def check_available_credit_C12(ws_auth_card_number: str, ws_auth_amount: Decimal, card_account_file_read: callable, ws_available_credit: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_search_key = ws_auth_card_number
    ws_card_account_rec = card_account_file_read(ws_search_key)
    if ws_available_credit >= ws_auth_amount:
        ws_credit_available = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_credit_available = 'N'
        ws_auth_decline_code = '51'
    return ws_credit_available, ws_auth_decline_code

def approve_auth_C12(generate_auth_code: callable, record_authorization: callable, ws_auth_amount: Decimal, ws_available_credit: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_auth_response_code = '00'
    ws_auth_code = generate_auth_code()
    ws_available_credit -= ws_auth_amount
    record_authorization(ws_auth_code)
    return ws_available_credit

        STRING 'ERROR: ' DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_error_code DELIMITED SIZE

               ws_error_msg DELIMITED SIZE"""
           INTO ws_formatted_error"""

    print(WS_FORMATTED_ERROR)"""

def treasury_management_C13() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    calculate_cash_position_C13()
    project_cash_flows_C13()
    manage_reserves_C13()
    manage_investments_C13()
    manage_borrowings_C13()

def calculate_cash_position_C13() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_treasury_management
    ws_treasury_management.ws_cash_position = Decimal("0.00")
    sum_vault_cash_C13()
    sum_fed_account_C13()
    sum_correspondent_balances_C13()

def sum_vault_cash_C13() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, ws_treasury_management
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        vault_rec = read_vault_cash_file()
        if vault_rec is None:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_treasury_management.ws_cash_position += vault_rec.vault_balance
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def sum_fed_account_C13() -> None:
    """
    32120-sum_fed_account"""
        READ fed_account_file INTO ws_fed_balance"""
        ADD ws_fed_balance TO ws_cash_position"""
    """
    global ws_treasury_management
    fed_account = read_fed_account_file()
    ws_treasury_management.ws_cash_position += fed_account.ws_fed_balance"""
""""""
def sum_correspondent_balances_C13() -> None:"""
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, ws_treasury_management
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        corr_rec = read_correspondent_file()
        if corr_rec is None:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_treasury_management.ws_cash_position += corr_rec.corr_balance
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def project_cash_flows_C13() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_treasury_management
    ws_treasury_management.ws_projected_inflows = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_treasury_management.ws_projected_outflows = Decimal("0.00")
    project_loan_payments_C13()
    project_deposit_flows_C13()
    project_investment_maturities_C13()
    ws_treasury_management.ws_net_position = ws_treasury_management.ws_cash_position + ws_treasury_management.ws_projected_inflows - ws_treasury_management.ws_projected_outflows

def project_loan_payments_C13() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, ws_treasury_management
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        loan_pmt_rec = read_loan_schedule_file()
        if loan_pmt_rec is None:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            if loan_pmt_rec.loan_pmt_date <= WS_PROJECTION_DATE:
                ws_treasury_management.ws_projected_inflows += loan_pmt_rec.loan_pmt_amount
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def project_deposit_flows_C13() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_EXPECTED_DEPOSITS, WS_EXPECTED_WITHDRAWALS, ws_treasury_management
    WS_EXPECTED_DEPOSITS = WS_AVG_DAILY_DEPOSITS * WS_PROJECTION_DAYS
    WS_EXPECTED_WITHDRAWALS = WS_AVG_DAILY_WITHDRAWALS * WS_PROJECTION_DAYS
    ws_treasury_management.ws_projected_inflows += WS_EXPECTED_DEPOSITS
    ws_treasury_management.ws_projected_outflows += None  # TODO: was WS_EXPECT

def update_cfp_document_C14() -> None:
    """33330-update_cfp_document."""
    global WS_CFP_STATUS, WS_AVAILABLE_FUNDING, WS_STRESSED_OUTFLOWS
    WS_CFP_DOCUMENT.WS_CFP_UPDATE_DATE = date.today()  # MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO ws_cfp_update_date
    WS_CFP_DOCUMENT.CFP_OVERALL_STATUS = WS_CFP_STATUS  # MOVE ws_cfp_status TO cfp_overall_status
    WS_CFP_DOCUMENT.WS_AVAILABLE_FUNDING = WS_AVAILABLE_FUNDING  # MOVE ws_available_funding TO cfp_total_sources
    WS_CFP_DOCUMENT.WS_STRESSED_OUTFLOWS = WS_STRESSED_OUTFLOWS  # MOVE ws_stressed_outflows TO cfp_stress_needs

    #REWRITE cfp_record FROM ws_cfp_document
    # In Python, you would likely append the data to a file or database
    # For demonstration, let's assume appending to a file:'
    cfp_data = f"{WS_CFP_DOCUMENT.CFP_OVERALL_STATUS},{WS_CFP_DOCUMENT.WS_AVAILABLE_FUNDING},{WS_CFP_DOCUMENT.WS_STRESSED_OUTFLOWS},{WS_CFP_DOCUMENT.WS_CFP_UPDATE_DATE}
"

    try:
        with open(CFP_RECORD_FILE, "a") as file: #Append
            file.write(cfp_data)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error writing to file: {e}")

def capital_management_C14() -> None:
    """34000-capital_management."""COBOL logic"""34100-calculate_capital_ratios."""COBOL logic"""34110-calculate_tier1."""
    global WS_TIER1_CAPITAL
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL = Decimal("0.00")  # MOVE ZEROES TO ws_tier1_capital
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += WS_COMMON_STOCK  # ADD ws_common_stock TO ws_tier1_capital
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS  # ADD ws_retained_earnings TO ws_tier1_capital
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += WS_AOCI  # ADD ws_aoci TO ws_tier1_capital
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= WS_GOODWILL  # SUBTRACT ws_goodwill FROM ws_tier1_capital
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= WS_INTANGIBLES  # SUBTRACT ws_intangibles FROM ws_tier1_capital
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= WS_DTA_DEDUCTION  # SUBTRACT ws_dta_deduction FROM ws_tier1_capital

def calculate_tier2_C14() -> None:
    """34120-calculate_tier2."""
    global WS_TIER2_CAPITAL, WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL = Decimal("0.00")  # MOVE ZEROES TO ws_tier2_capital
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL += WS_SUB_DEBT  # ADD ws_sub_debt TO ws_tier2_capital
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL += WS_ALLL_ELIGIBLE  # ADD ws_alll_eligible TO ws_tier2_capital
    WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL = WS_TIER1_CAPITAL + WS_TIER2_CAPITAL  # COMPUTE ws_total_capital = ws_tier1_capital + ws_tier2_capital

def calculate_ratios_C14() -> None:
    """34130-calculate_ratios."""COBOL logic"""34200-risk_weighted_assets."""
    global WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS = Decimal("0.00")  # MOVE ZEROES TO ws_risk_weighted_assets
    credit_rwa_C14()  # PERFORM 34210-credit_rwa
    market_rwa_C14()  # PERFORM 34220-market_rwa
    operational_rwa_C14()  # PERFORM 34230-operational_rwa

def credit_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """34210-credit_rwa."""
    global WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS, WS_CASH_POSITION, WS_GOVT_SECURITIES, WS_BANK_DEPOSITS, WS_RESIDENTIAL_MORTGAGES, WS_COMMERCIAL_LOANS, WS_CONSUMER_LOANS
    WS_CASH_RWA: Decimal = WS_CASH_POSITION * Decimal("0.00")  # COMPUTE ws_cash_rwa = ws_cash_position * 0.00
    WS_GOVT_RWA: Decimal = WS_GOVT_SECURITIES * Decimal("0.00")  # COMPUTE ws_govt_rwa = ws_govt_securities * 0.00
    WS_BANK_RWA: Decimal = WS_BANK_DEPOSITS * Decimal("0.20")  # COMPUTE ws_bank_rwa = ws_bank_deposits * 0.20
    WS_MORTGAGE_RWA: Decimal = WS_RESIDENTIAL_MORTGAGES * Decimal("0.50")  # COMPUTE ws_mortgage_rwa = ws_residential_mortgages * 0.50
    WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA: Decimal = WS_COMMERCIAL_LOANS * Decimal("1.00")  # COMPUTE ws_commercial_rwa = ws_commercial_loans * 1.00
    WS_CONSUMER_RWA: Decimal = WS_CONSUMER_LOANS * Decimal("1.00")

def main():
    """Main entry point"""
    global WS_GL_CONTROL_BAL, WS_SUBLEDGER_TOTAL, WS_RECON_DIFF

    WS_RECON_DIFF = WS_GL_CONTROL_BAL - WS_SUBLEDGER_TOTAL
    if WS_RECON_DIFF != Decimal("0"):
        log_recon_exception_C15()

def log_recon_exception_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL: 37235-log_recon_exception"""
    global WS_RECON_EXCEPTION, WS_GL_ACCOUNT, WS_RECON_DIFF, RECON_EXCEPTION_RECORD

    ws_recon_exception = WS_RECON_EXCEPTION()
    ws_recon_exception.RECON_EXC_ACCOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_GL_ACCOUNT
    ws_recon_exception.RECON_EXC_DIFF  = None  # TODO: was WS_RECON_DIFF
    ws_recon_exception.RECON_EXC_DATE = datetime.now().isoformat()
    # Assuming RECON_exception_record is a file write operation
    print(f"Writing to {RECON_EXCEPTION_RECORD}: {ws_recon_exception}")

def intercompany_recon_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL: 37300-intercompany_recon"""
    load_ic_balances_C15()
    match_ic_pairs_C15()
    report_ic_differences_C15()

def load_ic_balances_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL: 37310-load_ic_balances"""
    global WS_IC_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, INTERCOMPANY_FILE, WS_IC_BALANCE_VAR, WS_IC_ARRAY

    WS_IC_COUNT = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # Added initialization

    # Simulating reading from a file
    intercompany_data = [
        WS_IC_BALANCE(IC_FROM_ENTITY="ENTITY1", IC_TO_ENTITY="ENTITY2", IC_AMOUNT=Decimal("100.00")),
        WS_IC_BALANCE(IC_FROM_ENTITY="ENTITY2", IC_TO_ENTITY="ENTITY1", IC_AMOUNT=Decimal("-100.00")),
        WS_IC_BALANCE(IC_FROM_ENTITY="ENTITY3", IC_TO_ENTITY="ENTITY4", IC_AMOUNT=Decimal("50.00")),
    ]  # Example data

    for ws_ic_balance in intercompany_data: # Simulate reading file
        if WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y':
            break

        WS_IC_COUNT += 1
        WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_COUNT - 1] = ws_ic_balance
        
        if WS_IC_COUNT >= len(WS_IC_ARRAY):
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # This needs to be set each loop, or will break right away

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    print(f"Loaded {WS_IC_COUNT} intercompany balances.")

def match_ic_pairs_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL: 37320-match_ic_pairs"""
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_IC_COUNT

    WS_IC_IDX = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        find_ic_counterpart_C15(WS_IC_IDX)
        WS_IC_IDX += 1

def find_ic_counterpart_C15(ws_ic_idx: int) -> None:
    """COBOL: 37325-find_ic_counterpart"""
    global WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_IDX2, WS_IC_COUNT, WS_IC_DIFF, WS_IC_ARRAY

    WS_SEARCH_FROM = WS_IC_ARRAY[ws_ic_idx - 1].IC_FROM_ENTITY  #Access array properly
    WS_SEARCH_TO = WS_IC_ARRAY[ws_ic_idx - 1].IC_TO_ENTITY #Access array properly

    WS_IC_IDX2 = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX2 <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        if WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_FROM_ENTITY == WS_SEARCH_TO: #Access array properly
            if WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_TO_ENTITY == WS_SEARCH_FROM: #Access array properly
                WS_IC_DIFF = WS_IC_ARRAY[ws_ic_idx - 1].IC_AMOUNT + WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_AMOUNT #Access array properly
                if WS_IC_DIFF != Decimal("0.00"):
                    log_ic_diff_C15()
                break  # EXIT PERFORM
        WS_IC_IDX2 += 1

def log_ic_diff_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL: 37326-log_ic_diff"""

    ws_ic_diff_rec.ICD_FROM  = None  # TODO: was WS_SEARCH_FROM
    ws_ic_diff_rec.ICD_TO  = None  # TODO: was WS_SEARCH_TO
    ws_ic_diff_rec.ICD_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_IC_DIFF
    # Assuming IC_DIFF_RECORD is a file write operation
    print(f"Writing to {IC_DIFF_RECORD}: {ws_ic_diff_rec}")

def move_to_archive_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL: 38410-move_to_archive"""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, AUDIT_FILE, WS_AUDIT_RECORD, WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP, WS_ARCHIVE_DATE, ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    # Simulate reading from AUDIT_FILE
    audit_data = [
        WS_AUDIT_RECORD(WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP="2024-01-01"),
        WS_AUDIT_RECORD(WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP="2024-02-01"),
        WS_AUDIT_RECORD(WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP="2024-03-01")
    ] # Example data

    for ws_audit_record in audit_data:
        if WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y':
            break
        
        if ws_audit_record.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP < WS_ARCHIVE_DATE:
            # Assuming ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD is a file write operation
            print(f"Writing to {ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD}: {ws_audit_record}")
            # Simulate deleting from AUDIT_FILE (not possible to truly delete in this context)
        
        if len(audit_data) > 0:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        else:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def transaction_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL: 39140-transaction_metrics"""
    global WS_TPS, WS_TRANS_COUNT, WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS, WS_AVG_RESPONSE, WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME

    WS_TPS = Decimal(WS_TRANS_COUNT) / Decimal(WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS) if WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS else Decimal("0.00")
    WS_AVG_RESPONSE = Decimal(WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME) / Decimal(WS_TRANS_COUNT) if WS_TRANS_COUNT else Decimal("0.00")

def analyze_performance_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL: 39200-analyze_performance"""
    global WS_AVG_RESPONSE, WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD, WS_PERF_DEGRADED, WS_TPS, WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD, WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW

    if WS_AVG_RESPONSE > WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD:
        WS_PERF_DEGRADED = 'Y'
    if WS_TPS < WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD:
        WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW = 'Y'
